## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- kserve (also known as\: KServe) is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 17 CRD(s)\: ClusterStorageContainer, KernelCacheCapture, KernelCacheNodeGroup, KernelCacheNode, KernelCache, LLMInferenceServiceConfig, LLMInferenceService, LocalModelCache, LocalModelNamespaceCache, LocalModelNodeGroup, LocalModelNode, ClusterServingRuntime, InferenceGraph, InferenceService, ServingRuntime, TrainedModel, InferencePool.
- deploying 4 workload(s) (kserve-controller-manager, llmisvc-controller-manager, kserve-localmodel-controller-manager, kserve-module-controller-manager) with 4 container(s).
- watching 48 resource type(s).
- exposing 6 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8443.
- with 25 webhook(s) (15 validating, 6 mutating).
- protected by 5 network policy/policies.
- connecting to external services\: azure-blob, gcs, httpx, requests, s3, urllib, weaviate.
- runtime dependencies\: OpenTelemetry Collector, knative-local-gateway, kubernetes.
- defining 72 API spec type(s) across 146 struct(s) with 479 total fields.
- with 2128 operator config constants (28 image references, 17 env vars).
- following 14 reconciliation steps across 11 controller(s).
- reporting 47 status condition types.
- with 3 ingress route(s).
- Python k8s client with 85 API call(s) and 1 CRD kind reference(s).
- integrates with Istio, KServe, Kueue, Prometheus, management relationship via labels/annotations.
- deploys kube-auth-proxy for authentication.
- kustomize overlays\: config/base, config/certmanager/kserve, config/certmanager, config/certmanager/llmisvc, config/certmanager/localmodel, config/clusterresources, config/components/kserve,
